#IMPORT [DAY,1, RICE1_DAY] AS DAYTREND
#IMPORT [HOUR,1, 米仓I号小时趋势] AS HOURTREND
#IMPORT [MIN,1, 米仓I号多周期MACD] AS MULTIMACD
#IMPORT [MIN,1, 米仓I号追踪区域] AS TRACEAREA
#IMPORT [MIN,45, 米仓I号单周期MACD] AS MACDIMPORT25
#IMPORT [MIN,1, 米仓I号变量赋值] AS VARS

//PB1:(EMA(CLOSE,M1)+MA(CLOSE,M1*2)+MA(CLOSE,M1*4))/3;
//PB2:(EMA(CLOSE,M2)+MA(CLOSE,M2*2)+MA(CLOSE,M2*4))/3;
//PB3:(EMA(CLOSE,M3)+MA(CLOSE,M3*2)+MA(CLOSE,M3*4))/3;
//PB4:(EMA(CLOSE,M4)+MA(CLOSE,M4*2)+MA(CLOSE,M4*4))/3;
//PB5:(EMA(CLOSE,M5)+MA(CLOSE,M5*2)+MA(CLOSE,M5*4))/3;

BP;
NUMOFDAY: BARPOS, NODRAW;

BUYVOL:= DAYTREND.SCALEBUYVOL, PRECIS0, NODRAW;//主动买
SALEVOL:= DAYTREND.SCALESALEVOL, PRECIS0, NODRAW;//主动卖
DAYBUYSCALE: DAYTREND.SCALEBUY, PRECIS3, COLORRED,  NODRAW;
DAYSALESCALE: DAYTREND.SCALESALE, PRECIS3, COLORGREEN, NODRAW;

NUMOFNEWDAY:  BARSLAST(DATE <> REF(DATE, 1)) + 1, COLORWHITE, NODRAW;
NUMOFNEWHOUR:= BARSLAST(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)) + 1, COLORWHITE, NODRAW;
NUMOFPREHOUR:= SUMBARS(NUMOFNEWHOUR = 1, 2), COLORWHITE, NODRAW ;
NUMOF2HOURSAGO:= SUMBARS(NUMOFNEWHOUR = 1, 3), COLORWHITE, NODRAW ;
NUMOF3HOURSAGO:= SUMBARS(NUMOFNEWHOUR = 1, 4), COLORWHITE, NODRAW ;

ISWEAKUPTRENDOFHOUROCCURED := REF(HOURTREND.CURRENTTREND, NUMOFNEWHOUR) = 3 && REF(HOURTREND.ISWEAKUPTREND, NUMOFNEWHOUR) = 0, NODRAW;

NUMOFYESTERDAY : SUMBARS(NUMOFNEWDAY = 1, 2), COLORWHITE, NODRAW ; //取前一日开盘的K线到当前的周期
NUMOF2DAYSAGO := SUMBARS(NUMOFNEWDAY = 1, 3), COLORWHITE, NODRAW ;//取前两日开盘的K线到当前的周期
NUMOF3DAYSAGO := SUMBARS(NUMOFNEWDAY = 1, 4), COLORWHITE, NODRAW ;//取前三日开盘的K线到当前的周期
//5MOPEN : IF(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 0, OPEN, 0), NODRAW;
//5MCLOSE: IF(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4, CLOSE, 0), NODRAW;

ISSKIPDAY : HOURTREND.ISSKIPDAY, NODRAW;
小时周期: HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, NODRAW;
//假期首个4H : IF(REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) < 7, REF(HOURTREND.KTIME, 1) = 1030 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 0), NODRAW;
NUMOF4HSHIFT : IF(ISSKIPDAY = 1, IF(REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)  < 7, REF(HOURTREND.KTIME, 1) = 1030 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 0), IF(REF(ISSKIPDAY, 1) = 1, HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), MOD(REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1),  4) = 0) && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)) , COLORWHITE, NODRAW;

MAINTREND1M:EMA(EMA(CLOSE,3),3), PRECIS3;


TR := MAX(MAX((HIGH-LOW),ABS(REF(CLOSE,1)-HIGH)),ABS(REF(CLOSE,1)-LOW));//求最高价减去最低价，一个周期前的收盘价减去最高价的绝对值，一个周期前的收盘价减去最低价的绝对值，这三个值中的最大值
ATR : MA(TR,14), NODRAW;//求N个周期内的TR的简单移动平均

TOTALKDATANUM := TOTALKDATANUM + 1;
//MACD1
DIFF1:= EMA(CLOSE, 12) - EMA(CLOSE, 26), PRECIS2, COLORWHITE, NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA1 := EMA(DIFF1,9), PRECIS2, COLORWHITE, NODRAW;//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均
MACD1 := 30 * (DIFF1-DEA1), PRECIS2, COLORWHITE, NODRAW;

//多周期MACD
//MACD3
DIFF3:= EMA(CLOSE, 36) - EMA(CLOSE, 78), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA3:= EMA(DIFF3,27), NODRAW;//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均
//MACD3: 30 * (DIFF3-DEA3), NODRAW;
//} OF MACD3

//MACD 25
DIFF25:= EMA(CLOSE,288) - EMA(CLOSE,624), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA25:= EMA(DIFF25,216), NODRAW;
//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均
MACD25:= 2*(DIFF25-DEA25), NODRAW;

//} OF MACD 25

//MACD 30
DIFF30:= EMA(CLOSE,360) - EMA(CLOSE,780), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA30:= EMA(DIFF30,270), NODRAW;
//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均
//MACD30:= 2 * (DIFF30-DEA30), NODRAW;
MACD30:= MACDIMPORT25.MACD1, NODRAW;

DIFF60F:= EMA(CLOSE, 50 * 6) - EMA(CLOSE, 50 * 13), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA60F:= EMA(DIFF60F, 50 * 5), NODRAW;
//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均
MACD60F:= 2 * (DIFF60F-DEA60F), NODRAW;

//DIFF60:= EMA(EMA(CLOSE,60), 12) - EMA(EMA(CLOSE,60), 26), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
//DEA60:= EMA(DIFF60, 9), NODRAW;
DIFF60:= EMA(CLOSE, 49.5 * 12) - EMA(CLOSE, 49.5 * 26), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA60:= EMA(DIFF60, 49.5 * 9), NODRAW;
MACD60:= 2 * (DIFF60-DEA60), NODRAW;

//MACD5
//MACD5: 30 * (DIFF5-DEA5), NODRAW;
//} OF MACD5
//MACD5:= 2 * (DIFF5-DEA5),  PRECIS2, NODRAW;
CLOSE5:= REF(CLOSE, 4), NODRAW; //"MIN$5$CLOSE", NODRAW;
//MACD5
DIFF5M:= EMA(CLOSE5, 12) - EMA(CLOSE5, 26), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA5M:= EMA(DIFF5M,9), NODRAW;//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均
DIFF5:= EMA(CLOSE, 60) - EMA(CLOSE, 130), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA5:= EMA(DIFF5,45), NODRAW;//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均
MACD5:= 2 * (DIFF5-DEA5), PRECIS2, NODRAW;
//} OF MACD5
//MACD5:= 2 * (DIFF5M-DEA5M),  PRECIS2, NODRAW;

CLOSE5H:= REF(CLOSE, 299), NODRAW; //"HOUR$4$CLOSE", NODRAW; //
DIFF300:= EMA(CLOSE5H, 2400) - EMA(CLOSE5H, 5200), NODRAW;//短周期与长周期的收盘价的指数平滑移动平均值做差。
DEA300:= EMA(DIFF300, 1800), NODRAW;//DIFF的M个周期指数平滑移动平均

DIFF5H:= EMA(CLOSE5H, 12) - EMA(CLOSE5H, 26), NODRAW;
DEA5H:= EMA(DIFF5H,9), NODRAW;
//MACD5: 30 * (DIFF5-DEA5), NODRAW;
//} OF MACD5
MACD300:= 2 * (DIFF300-DEA300),  PRECIS2, NODRAW;
//MACD300:= 2 * (DIFF5H-DEA5H),  PRECIS2, NODRAW;
MACD3:= 2 * (DIFF3-DEA3), NODRAW;

MACD3MIX:= 2 * (DIFF3-DEA3) *  IF(((DIFF3-DEA3) > 0 && (DIFF5-DEA5) > 0) || ((DIFF3-DEA3) < 0 && (DIFF5-DEA5) < 0), 1, -1), NODRAW;
//} OF MACD5
MACD35:= 2 * (DIFF5-DEA5) + 2 * (DIFF3-DEA3) *  IF(((DIFF3-DEA3) > 0 && (DIFF5-DEA5) > 0) || ((DIFF3-DEA3) < 0 && (DIFF5-DEA5) < 0), 1, -1), NODRAW;



ISUPTREND := MAINTREND1M >= REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && MAINTREND1M > REF(MAINTREND1M,1) && MAINTREND1M - REF(MAINTREND1M,1) >= 0.618 && CLOSE > HV(CLOSE, 3)  && IF(ISUP, 1, IF(ISDOWN && CLOSE < MAINTREND1M, 0, 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
ISUPTRENDOVER0 := MAINTREND1M >= REF(MAINTREND1M,5)  && MAINTREND1M - REF(MAINTREND1M,1) >= 0 && CLOSE < LLV(HIGH, 3) && CLOSE <= LLV(CLOSE, 3) && (ISDOWN || OPEN= CLOSE) && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) < BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
ISUPTRENDOVER1 := MAINTREND1M >= REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && REF(MAINTREND1M,1) - MAINTREND1M > 0 && REF(MAINTREND1M,1) - MAINTREND1M < 5 && MAINTREND1M > REF(MAINTREND1M,3) && CLOSE < LLV(HIGH, 3) && CLOSE <= LLV(CLOSE, 3) && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) < BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
ISUPTRENDOVER := MAINTREND1M >= REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && MAINTREND1M <= REF(MAINTREND1M,1) && MAINTREND1M <= REF(MAINTREND1M,3) && REF(MAINTREND1M,1) - MAINTREND1M > 0.618 && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) < BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND), PRECIS0, NODRAW;


ISDOWNTREND := MAINTREND1M < REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && MAINTREND1M <= REF(MAINTREND1M,1) && REF(MAINTREND1M,1) - MAINTREND1M > 0.618 && CLOSE < LV(CLOSE, 3) && IF(ISDOWN, 1, IF(ISUP && CLOSE> MAINTREND1M, 0, 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
ISDOWNTRENDOVER0 := LLV(MAINTREND1M, 120) = MAINTREND1M && MAINTREND1M < REF(MAINTREND1M,5)  && MAINTREND1M - REF(MAINTREND1M,1) <= 0 && CLOSE >= HHV(LOW, 3) && CLOSE >= HHV(CLOSE, 3) && CLOSE > LLV(MAINTREND1M, 120) && (ISUP || OPEN= CLOSE) && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
ISDOWNTRENDOVER1 := MAINTREND1M < REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && MAINTREND1M - REF(MAINTREND1M,1) > 0 && MAINTREND1M - REF(MAINTREND1M,1) < 5 && MAINTREND1M <= REF(MAINTREND1M,3) 
	                         && HIGH = HHV(HIGH, 3) && CLOSE >= HHV(CLOSE, 3) && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
ISDOWNTRENDOVER := MAINTREND1M < REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && MAINTREND1M > REF(MAINTREND1M,1) && MAINTREND1M > REF(MAINTREND1M,3) &&  MAINTREND1M - REF(MAINTREND1M,1) > 0.618 && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND), PRECIS0, NODRAW;


//小时的主趋势线在多等状态下仍然在升高，但增速减缓
ISWEAKUPTREND := ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 0 && MAINTREND1M >= REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && MAINTREND1M > REF(MAINTREND1M,1) && MAINTREND1M - REF(MAINTREND1M,1) > 0, PRECIS0, NODRAW;

//小时的主趋势线仍然在降低，但降速减缓
ISWEAKDOWNTREND :=  ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 0 && MAINTREND1M < REF(MAINTREND1M,5) && MAINTREND1M < REF(MAINTREND1M,1) && REF(MAINTREND1M,1) - MAINTREND1M > 0, PRECIS0, NODRAW;

//空
IF ISDOWNTREND = 1
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := -4;
END

//空等
IF ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 0 && ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 0 && BARSLAST(ISDOWNTRENDOVER) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTRENDOVER) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && ISWEAKDOWNTREND = 1
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := -3.5;
END

//空等
IF ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 0 && ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 0 && BARSLAST(ISDOWNTRENDOVER) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTRENDOVER) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && ISWEAKDOWNTREND = 0
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := -3;
END


//空平
IF ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 1
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := -1;
END

//空平等
IF ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 0 && ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 0 && BARSLAST(ISDOWNTRENDOVER) < BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) > BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND)
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := -2;
END

//多
IF ISUPTREND = 1
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := 4;
END

//多等
IF ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 0 && ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 0 && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) < BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTRENDOVER) > BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) && ISWEAKUPTREND = 1
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := 3.5;
END

//多等
IF ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 0 && ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 0 && BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) < BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTRENDOVER) > BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) && ISWEAKUPTREND = 0
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := 3;
END

//多平
IF ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 1
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := 1;
END

//多平等
IF ISUPTREND = 0 && ISUPTRENDOVER = 0 && ISDOWNTREND = 0 && ISDOWNTRENDOVER = 0 && BARSLAST(ISUPTRENDOVER) < BARSLAST(ISUPTREND) && BARSLAST(ISUPTRENDOVER) < BARSLAST(ISDOWNTREND)
THEN
BEGIN
	CURRENTTREND := 2;
END

当前分钟趋势: CURRENTTREND, PRECIS0, NODRAW;

						   IF(CURRENTLOWESTTREND35 < MIN1(PRELOWESTTREND35, PRE2LOWESTTREND35, PRE3LOWESTTREND35, PRE4LOWESTTREND35, PRE5LOWESTTREND35, PRE6LOWESTTREND35), 6,  
						   IF(CURRENTLOWESTTREND35 < MIN1(PRELOWESTTREND35, PRE2LOWESTTREND35, PRE3LOWESTTREND35, PRE4LOWESTTREND35, PRE5LOWESTTREND35), 5,  
						   IF(CURRENTLOWESTTREND35 < MIN1(PRELOWESTTREND35, PRE2LOWESTTREND35, PRE3LOWESTTREND35, PRE4LOWESTTREND35), 4, 
						   IF(CURRENTLOWESTTREND35 < MIN1(PRELOWESTTREND35, PRE2LOWESTTREND35, PRE3LOWESTTREND35), 3,
						   IF(CURRENTLOWESTTREND35 < MIN1(PRELOWESTTREND35, PRE2LOWESTTREND35), 2,
						   IF(CURRENTLOWESTTREND35 < PRELOWESTTREND35, 1,0)))))))))))))))), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
HIGHERCOUNTER := COUNT(MAINTREND1M > PREHIGHESTTREND35 && REF(MAINTREND1M, 1) <= REF(PREHIGHESTTREND35, 1) && REF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35, 1) <= REF(PREHIGHESTTREND35, 1), NUMOFDAY - NUMOFMACD35CROSSUP), NODRAW;
COUNTERFLAGHIGHERERHIGHTREND := IF((MACD5 > 0 && MAINTREND1M > PREHIGHESTTREND35 && REF(MAINTREND1M, 1) <= REF(PREHIGHESTTREND35, 1) && REF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35, 1) <= REF(PREHIGHESTTREND35, 1) && HIGHERCOUNTER = 1)
								 || (MACD5 > 0 && REF(MACD5, 1) < 0 && MAINTREND1M > PREHIGHESTTREND35), 1, 0), NODRAW;
NUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND : IF((CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > PREHIGHESTTREND35 && MAINTREND1M > PREHIGHESTTREND35),
			                 //|| (CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 < PREHIGHESTTREND35 && MAINTREND1M > MAX(PRELOWESTTREND35, PRE2LOWESTTREND35) && MAINTREND1M > OPENMACD35CROSSUP), //多头回调后的反弹，如果高于前两次死叉下轨且1分钟主趋势线金叉MACD5 > 0的开盘价，可保持突破次数
						   COUNT(COUNTERFLAGHIGHERERHIGHTREND = 1, BARSLAST(MACD5 < 0 && CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 <= PREHIGHESTTREND35)), 0), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND : IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35, PRE10HIGHESTTREND35, PRE11HIGHESTTREND35, PRE12HIGHESTTREND35, PRE13HIGHESTTREND35, PRE14HIGHESTTREND35, PRE15HIGHESTTREND35, PRE16HIGHESTTREND35), 16, 
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35, PRE10HIGHESTTREND35, PRE11HIGHESTTREND35, PRE12HIGHESTTREND35, PRE13HIGHESTTREND35, PRE14HIGHESTTREND35, PRE15HIGHESTTREND35), 15, 
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35, PRE10HIGHESTTREND35, PRE11HIGHESTTREND35, PRE12HIGHESTTREND35, PRE13HIGHESTTREND35, PRE14HIGHESTTREND35), 14, 
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35, PRE10HIGHESTTREND35, PRE11HIGHESTTREND35, PRE12HIGHESTTREND35, PRE13HIGHESTTREND35), 13, 
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35, PRE10HIGHESTTREND35, PRE11HIGHESTTREND35, PRE12HIGHESTTREND35), 12, 
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35, PRE10HIGHESTTREND35, PRE11HIGHESTTREND35), 11, 
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35, PRE10HIGHESTTREND35), 10, 
						     IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35, PRE9HIGHESTTREND35), 9,
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35, PRE8HIGHESTTREND35), 8,		
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35, PRE7HIGHESTTREND35), 7,	
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35, PRE6HIGHESTTREND35), 6,	
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35, PRE5HIGHESTTREND35), 5,					  
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35, PRE4HIGHESTTREND35), 4, 
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35, PRE3HIGHESTTREND35), 3,
						     IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > MAX1(PREHIGHESTTREND35, PRE2HIGHESTTREND35), 2,
							 IF(CURRENTHIGHESTTREND35 > PREHIGHESTTREND35, 1,0)))))))))))))))), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

CURRENTLOWESTPRICEINDAY: LLV(LOW, NUMOFNEWDAY), PRECIS0, NODRAW ;

CLOSEOFHOUR: IF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY = REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), HOURTREND.KCLOSE, REF(HOURTREND.KCLOSE, 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
HOFHOUR:= IF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY = REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), HOURTREND.KHIGH, REF(HOURTREND.KHIGH, 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

//NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT : BARSLAST(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && MOD(REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 4) = 0), COLORWHITE, NODRAW;

LAST4HKOPEN: REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2) + 1), COLORWHITE, NODRAW;
LAST4HKCLOSE: REF(CLOSEOFHOUR, SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2)), COLORWHITE, NODRAW;
最近的黄线位移 : MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNY)) + 1, NODRAW;
前一个4K的位移:  SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2), NODRAW;

前一个5分钟的收盘价0: REF(CLOSE, SUMBARS(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4, 2) - 1), NODRAW;

ISINRIGHTTREND0 : (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && MAX(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) <  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)))
		       	 ||(MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && ((MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED)) && MAX(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) <  REF(CLOSEOFHOUR, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)) + 1)) || (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED)) && MAX(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) <  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED)) + 2)))  && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR))) //空头趋势启动阶段到青阴线，还未破小时120下轨，到蓝阴线，反弹转空要更敏感快速
				 , NODRAW;

ISINRIGHTTREND1 : (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && MAX(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) <  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)))
				 || (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && MAX(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) <  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)))

				 //(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER || IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER || IS4HTRENDCYAN || IS4HTRENDCYANRED || IS4HTRENDUCYAN || IS4HTRENDUCYANR) > BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW  || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR))
				 //|| (BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER || IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER || IS4HTRENDCYAN || IS4HTRENDCYANRED || IS4HTRENDUCYAN || IS4HTRENDUCYANR) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW  || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR) && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDORANGE))
//ISINRIGHTTREND1 : (BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND = 16) > BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && LAST4HKCLOSE <  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR)))
				, NODRAW;
ISINRIGHTTREND := ISINRIGHTTREND0 || ISINRIGHTTREND1, NODRAW;

//从空16反弹
ISINLEFTTREND0 : (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && MIN(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) >  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)))
		        ||(MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && MIN(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) >  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)))
		       	 //BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER || IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER || IS4HTRENDCYAN || IS4HTRENDCYANRED || IS4HTRENDUCYAN || IS4HTRENDUCYANR) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW  || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR)
				, NODRAW;

ISINLEFTTREND1 : (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && (MIN(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) >  REF(CLOSEOFHOUR, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED)) + 1) || MIN(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)) + 2)) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)))
				|| (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) && MIN(CLOSE, REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4))) >  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR)))				 
				, NODRAW;

ISINLEFTTREND := ISINLEFTTREND0 || ISINLEFTTREND1, NODRAW;

ISINLEFTRIGHTTREND : (ISINLEFTTREND && BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND = 16) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) && REF(CLOSE, SUMBARS(MOD(MINUTE, 5) = 4, 2)) <  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) + 2))

				, NODRAW;
//ISINLEFTTREND1 : (BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND = 16) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && LAST4HKCLOSE >  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED)) + 2) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDUCYANR)))
//, NODRAW;
//画4H阳线 - 黄线 16 + 120 HOUR HHV
IS4HTRENDYELLOW : (REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) = REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
					, NODRAW; // &&  REF(HOURTREND.ISHIGHERHIGHESTTREND, 1) = 1
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORYELLOW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW,CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORYELLOW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW,REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, COLORYELLOW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW,REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1),NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORYELLOW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW,REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2, BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORYELLOW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW,REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDYELLOW, MIN(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR),  (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORYELLOW;

//画4H阳线 - 黄缩头线
IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW : 	BARSLAST(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M = HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV) > BARSLAST(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M = HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV)
						&& ((REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)  && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) = REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //破前12高后缩头，破120HHV
							|| (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)  && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) = REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //破16后顶背离
							|| (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)  && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //大幅回调后的反弹，小时MACD<0， 小时趋势线还没再次突破120上轨
						), NODRAW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORYELLOW;

						//|| (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) <= 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)  && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) = REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //收盘低于前12高且低于120HHV
						//|| (BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && BARSLAST(CLOSE < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1)) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND = 16 && (IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDCYAN)) > BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) = REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //高位回调后反弹
						, NODRAW; // (REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))

//画4H阴线 - 浅蓝阴线 未破16 + 破120LLV  或者 破16 + 未破120LLV 或者 曾经破16收盘保持16 或者 曾经破16收盘已经归0

//画4H阳线 - 深橙线 破16 + 未破120HHV 或者 曾破16 + 未破120HHV, 但前面一定不能先出现深橙阴线 MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER)， BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN)) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW)) &&

					|| (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
					), NODRAW; // &&  REF(HOURTREND.ISHIGHERHIGHESTTREND, 1) = 1
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(200,68,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(200,68,0);

//画4H阴线 - 紫浅蓝阴线, 破8 + 未破120LLV
IS4HTRENDPLB : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0  && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1))
			   || (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0  && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1))//REF(HOURTREND.MACD5, 1) < 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1), NODRAW; //&& REF(HOURTREND.ISHIGHERHIGHESTTREND, 1) = 0 && REF(HOURTREND.ISLOWERLOWESTTREND, 1) = 1
			   //|| (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 &&  NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND > 0 && ISINRIGHTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1)) //虽然破前12小时最低后抽脚，但仍然没反弹到5分钟前下轨之上
				, NODRAW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1),NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, MIN(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
//阴影斜线下半部分
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(128,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 4, 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW * 3 / 4,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(128,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 2, 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 2,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(128,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) * 3 / 4, 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 4,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(128,128,255);

//阴影斜线上半部分
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 4,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) * 3 / 4, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(128,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 2, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(128,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW * 3 / 4,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDPLB, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 4, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(128,128,255);
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//画4H阳线 - 红线
//破空16后反弹未及多16且未出现黄阴橙阴
4HRED1 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && ISINLEFTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)), NODRAW; 
//破空16后反弹及多16或出现黄阴橙阴， 回调出现紫红阴后，再次反弹
4HRED2 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 0) > 0 && (BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) || BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16)) && ISINRIGHTTREND  && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && (CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED) + 2) || CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED) + 2)) && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 8) > 0 &&  REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 15) 
				, NODRAW;	
//高位16回调后的反弹，门槛暂定破4，考虑是否设置为绿阳线减少操作频率？
4HRED3 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && ISINRIGHTTREND  && BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 4, 15) > 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 4, 15) > 0 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR) //高位回调后出现底部破前12低长下引线的阳线，且破3反弹
				, NODRAW;	
//高位16回调后的反弹，突破前12高
4HRED4 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && ISINRIGHTTREND  && BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 4, 15) > 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 8) > 0 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1))  //高位回调后出现的阳线，破3反弹， 虽然未有底部破前12低长下引线
				, NODRAW;	
//浅蓝红线后的阳线
//是否最近出现浅蓝红线 : BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE) , NODRAW;
//最近浅蓝红线开盘价 : REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER) + 2), NODRAW;
4HRED5 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) <= 16 && NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND = 0 && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE) 
				&& HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER) + 2)) 
				, NODRAW;
		
//青红阴线后的阳线, 高于其收盘价及视为有效红线
最近青红阴线开盘价 :  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED) + 2), NODRAW;
是否最近青红阴线 : BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE) , NODRAW;
4HRED6 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) <= 16 && NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND = 0 && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE) 
				&& HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(CLOSE, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED) + 2))
				, NODRAW;
/*
是否最近红阴线 : BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE), NODRAW;
是否最近红阴线1 : BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDCYAN) > BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)  && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE), NODRAW;
最近红阴线的位置 : NUMOFDAY - BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED) - 1, NODRAW;
最近红阴线的位移 : BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED), NODRAW;
最近红阴线开盘价: REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED) + 2), NODRAW;
*/
//最近4小时收盘价: CLOSEOFHOUR, NODRAW;
//其他条件: REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16  && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 15) > 0 && NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND < 2, NODRAW;
//高位破6回调后的反弹，跟在红阴线后面，收盘高于红阴线开盘价， 或者高于前一个红线/淡黄线的收盘价
4HRED7 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16  && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 15) > 0 && NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND < 2 && ISINRIGHTTREND && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE) && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR 
			&& (CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED) + 2) || CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW) + 2))), NODRAW;

//橙线回调后的反弹， 突破到橙阴线，如果回调没有向下突破到紫兰阴线IS4HTRENDPLB， 在经过至少一个4小时K线周期后（很重要），当前4小时K线收盘价抽脚向上突破前橙阴线最低，则有望继续多头, 或者多个4小时周期后，当前4小时最低价突破最近橙线的最低价，抽脚向上突破
4HRED8 : (ISINRIGHTTREND && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW) && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 8) > 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 
				&& HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) 
				&& ((BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPLB), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) && (CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE) + 2) || CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE) + 2) || CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) + 2)))
					|| (BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) > SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2) && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPLB), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED)) && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) + 2) && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) + 2))
					|| (BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) && MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNDEEPOR), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE)) > SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 3) && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) < MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPLB), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED)) && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE)) + 2) && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE)) + 2)))				
			), NODRAW;			
//橙线回调后的反弹， 如果回调突破紫兰阴线了，则需要反向突破紫兰阴线开盘价才算是继续多头
4HRED9 : (ISINRIGHTTREND && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW)  && NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND = 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 8) > 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 
				&& HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) 
				&& (BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) > MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPLB), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED)) && CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPLB), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED)) + 2)) )
			, NODRAW;

//高位16横盘, 收盘高于上一个4小时收盘价
4HRED10 : (MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBACKYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNY), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNLY)) + 1 = SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2) && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && ISINLEFTTREND  && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 15) > 0 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR > LAST4HKCLOSE)
            , NODRAW;

4HRED11 : BARSLAST(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M = HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV) > BARSLAST(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M = HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV) && (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) = 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR  && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
			, NODRAW;	

IS4HTRENDRED:  4HRED1 || 4HRED2 || 4HRED3 || 4HRED4 || 4HRED5 || 4HRED6 || 4HRED7 || 4HRED8 || 4HRED9 || 4HRED10 || 4HRED11, NODRAW; //出现浅蓝红线后, 阳线且收盘价大于浅蓝红线收盘价
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, COLORRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED,REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2, BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORRED; //画5M最高价
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED,REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDRED, MIN(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORRED; //画5M最低价
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
ISDOWN4KNOTLAST4K : REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2) + 1) <= REF(CLOSEOFHOUR, SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2)), NODRAW;

//1. 距离低位破16更近， 期间破位最低小于16， 最低价高于前12小时最低价，收盘价低于前12小时最高价, 收盘价高于最近一次破16大阴线的开盘价
4HDOWNRED1 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && ISINLEFTTREND && ISDOWN4KNOTLAST4K && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 8 &&  HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYAN), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDCYANRED), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDULIGHTBLUER)) + 2)), NODRAW; 
//2. 破空16后反弹及多16或出现黄阴橙阴， 回调出现紫红阴后，再次反弹
4HDOWNRED2 : (BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && ISINRIGHTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT &&  REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED) + 2) && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 8) 
				, NODRAW;
//3. 高位破16后，位于淡黄线后，收盘高于淡黄线开盘价, 5分下轨破位不超过2
4HDOWNRED3 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 8 && NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND < 2 && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDCYAN) > BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW) && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE) > BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW) && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 &&  HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR &&  CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW) + 2) && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)), NODRAW;

//4. 高位破16小幅回调后未及紫阴线就反弹，位于红线后，收盘高于红线开盘价, 5分下轨破位不超过2
4HDOWNRED4 : (REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 8 && NUMOFLOWERLOWTREND < 2 && ISINRIGHTTREND && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 &&  HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR &&  CLOSEOFHOUR >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDRED) + 2) && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)), NODRAW;

//画4H阴线 - 红阴线
IS4HTRENDDOWNRED : 4HDOWNRED1 || 4HDOWNRED2 || 4HDOWNRED3 || 4HDOWNRED4, NODRAW;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1),NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTRED;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, MIN(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTRED;
//阴影斜线下半部分
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(255,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 4, 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW * 3 / 4,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(255,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 2, 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 2,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(255,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) * 3 / 4, 1,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, CLOSEOFHOUR, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 4,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(255,128,255);

//阴影斜线上半部分
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 4,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) * 3 / 4, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(255,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 2, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(255,128,255);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW * 3 / 4,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - (REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) - CLOSEOFHOUR) / 4, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK1, RGB(255,128,255);
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

//画4H阳线 - 橙线 破8 + 破前12高 + 未破120HHV 
IS4HTRENDORANGE : (BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && ISINLEFTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
					|| (BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 2, 15) > 0 && ISINLEFTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //破前12高后短暂回调
					|| (BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && ISINRIGHTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //高位回调后反弹
					, NODRAW; // &&  REF(HOURTREND.ISHIGHERHIGHESTTREND, 1) = 1
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE,REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2, BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0); //画5M最高价
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE,REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDORANGE, MIN(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0); //画5M最低价

//画4H阳线 - 橙缩头线 破8 + 破前12高 + 未破120HHV 
IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE : (BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && ISINLEFTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
					|| (BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 1) > 0 && ISINLEFTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //破前12高后短暂回调
					|| (BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 1) > 0 && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPRED) < BARSLAST(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 16) && ISINRIGHTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1)) //高位回调后反弹
					, NODRAW; // &&  REF(HOURTREND.ISHIGHERHIGHESTTREND, 1) = 1
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,128,0);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE,REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2, BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORGREEN; //画5M最高价
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE,REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE, MIN(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORGREEN; //画5M最低价

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//画4H阳线 - 浅橙线 爬升期 破5未破8 + 未破120HHV 或者 破8 + 未破前12高 + 未破120HHV
IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE : (BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) < 1 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 5, 8) > 0 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
					    || (BETWEEN(REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) < 1 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 5, 8) > 0 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
					    || (BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 8, 15) > 0 && ISINLEFTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDHHV, 1))
					    , NODRAW; // &&  REF(HOURTREND.ISHIGHERHIGHESTTREND, 1) = 1
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,188,128);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,188,128);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,188,128);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,188,128);
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE,REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2, BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,188,128); //画5M最高价
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE,REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDLIGHTORANGE, MIN(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, RGB(255,188,128); //画5M最低价

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//MACD25 < 0, 对应5分钟周期的REF(HOURTREND.MACD5, 1)
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
ISDOWNNREDABSENT : BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNRED) > BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE || IS4HTRENDDOWNY || IS4HTRENDDOWNLY || IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE), NODRAW;
ISUP16NOTLAST4K : MIN1(BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW), BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE)) > SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2), NODRAW;
//画4H阳线 - 浅绿线 
4HLIGHTGREEN1 : (IS4HTRENDRED = 0 && ISINRIGHTTREND && ISDOWNNREDABSENT && ISUP16NOTLAST4K && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) <= 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)  && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)) //绿色阳线未破前12低
				 , NODRAW;
4HLIGHTGREEN2 : (IS4HTRENDRED = 0 && ISINRIGHTTREND && ISDOWNNREDABSENT && ISUP16NOTLAST4K && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) < REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 15) > 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 4) > 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)  && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)) //绿色阳线破前12低，但反弹非常微弱  
				 , NODRAW;
4HLIGHTGREEN3 : (IS4HTRENDRED = 0 && ISINRIGHTTREND && ISUP16NOTLAST4K && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE || IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) < BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDYELLOW || IS4HTRENDLIGHTYELLOW)  && NUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND = 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 8) > 0 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 0) > 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 //橙线回调后反弹冲高后回落，破5分前上轨，并MACD5 > 0, 下一个4小时只要不破5分前上轨并回调就可能是个较大的空头趋势
						&& HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) <= CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1) && REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1) <= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1))
				 , NODRAW;
IS4HTRENDUPGREEN:  4HLIGHTGREEN1 || 4HLIGHTGREEN2 || 4HLIGHTGREEN3
					, NODRAW; //&&  REF(HOURTREND.ISHIGHERHIGHESTTREND, 1) = 1
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), 0,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, CLOSEOFHOUR, 0,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, CLOSEOFHOUR,NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN;
DRAWLINE3(BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN,REF(HOURTREND.4KHHV, 1), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2, BARTYPE = 1 && IS4HTRENDUPGREEN, MAX(REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1), CLOSEOFHOUR), (NUMSHIFTOF4HFORKDRAW + 1) / 2,0), LINETHICK5, COLORLIGHTGREEN; //画5M最高价

//画4H阴线 - 浅绿阴线 IS4HTRENDPURPLE
IS4HTRENDDOWNGREEN : (IS4HTRENDCYANRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER = 0 && IS4HTRENDPRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE = 0 && IS4HTRENDPURPLED = 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && ISINRIGHTTREND && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.4KLLV, 1) >= REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KLOW, 1) && CLOSEOFHOUR <  REF(HOURTREND.PRE12KHIGH, 1)) //右侧趋势
					|| (IS4HTRENDCYANRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER = 0 && IS4HTRENDPRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE = 0 && IS4HTRENDPURPLED = 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 7) > 0 && ISINRIGHTTREND && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1))//右侧趋势
					|| (IS4HTRENDCYANRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER = 0 && IS4HTRENDPRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE = 0 && IS4HTRENDPURPLED = 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BETWEEN(REF(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1), 0, 15) > 0 && ISINLEFTTREND && ISDOWN4KNOTLAST4K = 0 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1)) //左侧趋势，但前一个4小时是阴线
					|| (IS4HTRENDCYANRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER = 0 && IS4HTRENDPRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE = 0 && IS4HTRENDPURPLED = 0 &&  REF(IS4HTRENDRED, SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2)) = 1 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) > BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDCYAN) && ISINLEFTTREND && ISDOWN4KNOTLAST4K = 1 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR < LAST4HKOPEN && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1)) //左侧趋势，前一个4小时是红阳线， 但未及橙线, 当前4小时K线收盘价低于前一K线开盘价
			        || (IS4HTRENDCYANRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUER = 0 && IS4HTRENDPRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNRED = 0 && IS4HTRENDDOWNORANGE = 0 && IS4HTRENDPURPLED = 0 &&  REF(IS4HTRENDRED, SUMBARS(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT = 0, 2)) = 0 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFLOWERLOWTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && REF(HHV(ACTUALNUMOFHIGHERHIGHTREND, NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT), NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT + 1) < 16 && BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDORANGE || IS4HTRENDBACKORANGE || IS4HTRENDDEEPORANGE) > BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDBLUE || IS4HTRENDDLIGHTBLUE || IS4HTRENDCYAN) && ISINLEFTTREND && ISDOWN4KNOTLAST4K = 1 && HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1) && NUMOF4HSHIFT  && REF(HOURTREND.PRE3KOPEN, 1) > CLOSEOFHOUR && CLOSEOFHOUR < LAST4HKCLOSE && REF(HOURTREND.MAINTREND1M, 1) > REF(HOURTREND.120MAINTRENDLLV, 1)) //左侧趋势，前一个4小时是阳线， 但未及橙线, 也不是红阳线，当前4小时K线收盘价低于前一K线收盘价
, NODRAW;

NUMOFLASTDOWNPURPLEK4HOPEN : REF(NUMOFDAY, REF(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPURPLED)) + BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPURPLED)), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
NUMOFLASTDOWNPURPLEK4HCLOSE : REF(NUMOFDAY, 1 + BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDPURPLED)), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
LASTDOWNPURPLEK4HOURCLOSE: REF(CLOSE, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFLASTDOWNPURPLEK4HCLOSE), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

IS4HTRENDFIRSTPURPLE : IF(NUMOFLASTYELLOWK4HOPEN > NUMOFLASTORANGEK4HOPEN, IS4HTRENDPURPLED && COUNT(IS4HTRENDPURPLED, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFLASTYELLOWK4HOPEN) = 1, IS4HTRENDPURPLED && COUNT(IS4HTRENDPURPLED, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFLASTORANGEK4HOPEN = 1)), NODRAW;
NUMOFFIRSTDOWNPURPLEK4HOPEN : REF(NUMOFDAY, REF(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDFIRSTPURPLE)) + BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDFIRSTPURPLE)), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
FIRSTDOWNPURPLEK4HOUROPEN : REF(OPEN, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFFIRSTDOWNPURPLEK4HOPEN), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
NUMOFFIRSTDOWNPURPLEK4HCLOSE : REF(NUMOFDAY, 1 + BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDFIRSTPURPLE)), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
FIRSTDOWNPURPLEK4HOURCLOSE: REF(CLOSE, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFFIRSTDOWNPURPLEK4HCLOSE), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

IS4HTRENDFIRSTORANGE : IF(NUMOFLASTDOWNBLUEK4HOPEN > NUMOFLASTDOWNCYANK4HOPEN, IS4HTRENDORANGE && COUNT(IS4HTRENDORANGE, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFLASTDOWNBLUEK4HOPEN) = 1, IS4HTRENDORANGE && COUNT(IS4HTRENDORANGE, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFLASTDOWNCYANK4HOPEN) = 1), NODRAW;
NUMOFFIRSTDOWNORANGEK4HOPEN : REF(NUMOFDAY, REF(NUMSHIFTOF4HFORCOUNT, BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDFIRSTORANGE)) + BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDFIRSTORANGE)), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
FIRSTDOWNORANGEK4HOUROPEN : REF(OPEN, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFFIRSTDOWNORANGEK4HOPEN), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
NUMOFFIRSTDOWNORANGEK4HCLOSE : REF(NUMOFDAY, 1 + BARSLAST(IS4HTRENDFIRSTORANGE)), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
FIRSTDOWNORANGEK4HOURCLOSE: REF(CLOSE, NUMOFDAY - NUMOFFIRSTDOWNORANGEK4HCLOSE), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

NUMOFLASTYELLOWKHOPEN : IF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDYELLOW)), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDYELLOW) - BARSLAST(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)))), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
LASTYELLOWKHOUROPEN : IF(ISHOURTRENDYELLOW, OPENOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KOPEN, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDYELLOW) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
LASTYELLOWKHOURCLOSE: IF(ISHOURTRENDYELLOW, CLOSEOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KCLOSE, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDYELLOW) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

NUMOFLASTORKHOPEN : IF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDOR)), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDOR) - BARSLAST(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)))), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
LASTORKHOUROPEN : IF(ISHOURTRENDOR, OPENOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KOPEN, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDOR) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
LASTORKHOURCLOSE : IF(ISHOURTRENDOR, CLOSEOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KCLOSE, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDOR) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

NUMOFLASTDOWNBPURPLEKHOPEN : IF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDDOWNBPURPLE)), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDDOWNBPURPLE) - BARSLAST(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)))), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
LASTDOWNBPURPLEKHOUROPEN : IF(ISHOURTRENDDOWNBPURPLE, OPENOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KOPEN, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDDOWNBPURPLE) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
LASTDOWNBPURPLEKHOURCLOSE : IF(ISHOURTRENDDOWNBPURPLE, CLOSEOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KCLOSE, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDDOWNBPURPLE) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

NUMOFLASTDOWNBLUECYANKHOPEN : IF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUECYAN)), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUECYAN) - BARSLAST(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)))), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
LASTDOWNBLUECYANKHOUROPEN : IF(ISHOURTRENDBLUECYAN, OPENOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KOPEN, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUECYAN) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
LASTDOWNBLUECYANKHOURCLOSE : IF(ISHOURTRENDBLUECYAN, CLOSEOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KCLOSE, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUECYAN) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;

NUMOFLASTDOWNBLUEKHOPEN : IF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUE)), REF(NUMOFDAY, SUMBARS(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1), 2)  - 1 + BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUE) - BARSLAST(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY <> REF(HOURTREND.NUMOFNEWDAY, 1)))), PRECIS0, NODRAW; 
LASTDOWNBLUEKHOUROPEN : IF(ISHOURTRENDBLUE, OPENOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KOPEN, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUE) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
LASTDOWNBLUEKHOURCLOSE : IF(ISHOURTRENDBLUE, CLOSEOFHOUR, REF(HOURTREND.KCLOSE, BARSLAST(ISHOURTRENDBLUE) + 1)), PRECIS0, NODRAW;
*/

0.00000000000000000000000000 , COLORWHITE, NODRAW;
模型分隔符在这里分割两个模型 : 0.0,  COLORWHITE, NODRAW;
